Let's design final algorithm robust:
A. Include fast exact utilities.
B. Initial candidates:
   - parent resampled to selected lengths.
   - Structured inits including asymmetric shapes: downward ramp, upward ramp, two blocks with unequal masses/widths, smooth sigmoid steps, low-frequency random, Fourier basis random.
   - Low-dimensional optimization of parametric shapes (piecewise wheels) at n=2048/4096 to get refined starting points.
C. Main local optimizer:
   - For each target length (maybe 2048, 8192, 16384, 32768), run hybrid: LSE projected L-BFGS or custom adaptive gradient, plus LP active set for smaller lengths, plus active-weight subgradient.
D. Track global best across lengths, then final resample/refine at best length or slightly larger.

Let's design custom AmsGrad/Nesterov on simplex using LSE objective (high beta). It can be fast and allow many iterations.

Pseudo:
def _smooth_train(z, beta, deadline, method='adam'):
   z anchored, best_a/score.
   maintain momentum_t, velocity_t.

Structured inits:
def _make_inits(n, rng, parent):
  out=[]
  def add(arr): clip, sum, normalize, out.append if unique? To avoid duplicates, maybe check simple norm. Use cap 40.
  if parent: add(resample(parent,n)); add(reverse(resample...))
  x = np.linspace(0,1,n)
  add(np.ones(n))
  # asymmetric two blocks fill length
  for Lfrac in [0.5,0.6,0.7,0.75,0.8,0.85,0.9]:
     l1 = max(1,int(round(Lfrac*n)))
     l2 = max(1,n-l1)
     for p in [0.3,0.4,0.5,0.6,0.7,0.8]:
        arr=np.zeros(n); arr[:l1]=p/l1; arr[l1:]= (1-p)/l2; add(arr)
     # smooth transition: add exponential blend between left and right block values over width w
     for w in [0.02,0.05,0.1]: ...
  # linear ramps
  for amp in [0.1,0.3,0.6,1.0,1.5,2.0]:
     add(np.ones(n) + amp*(1-2*x)); add(np.ones(n)+amp*(2*x-1))
  # asymmetric center bump
  for ctr in [0.25,0.35,0.5,0.65,0.75]:
     for w in [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4]:
       arr=np.ones(n)+1.5*np.exp(-((x-ctr)/w)**2); add
  # U/V shapes
  for amp in [0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0]: add(1+amp*abs(2x-1)); add(1+amp*(1-abs(2x-1)**2)); add(1+amp*(0.5-x)? etc)
  # random Fourier low-freq
  for _ in range(8):
     signs = rng.standard_normal(n)
     # filter low freq: take random 1..K coefficients
     K=16; z=np.zeros(n)
     for f in range(1, K):
        z += rng.normal(0,1/(f**0.7))*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x + rng.normal(0,2*np.pi))
     add(np.exp(0.5*z))
  # random smooth via B-spline? Could generate random smooth z by low-pass: rng.standard_normal(n) convolved Gaussian width 10-50.
  for _ in range(6):
     width=rng.integers(5, max(6,n//100+5)); noise=rng.standard_normal(n); if width>1: noise=np.convolve(noise, np.ones(width)/width, mode='same'); add(np.exp(0.5*(noise-noise.mean())))
  Return out.

Detailed code for _lse_value_grad_log:
```
def _lse_value_grad_log(z, beta, anchor, vars_idx):
    n = len(vars_idx)+1
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)): return 1e100, zeros
    maxz = max(0.0, float(np.max(z)))
    p = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p[anchor] = np.exp(-maxz)
    p[vars_idx] = np.exp(z - maxz)
    S = float(p.sum())
    if S <= 0 or not finite: ...
    a = p / S
    # Maybe set tiny to 0? no.
    c = _fast_self_conv(a)
    m = c.size
    d = n * c
    dmax = float(d.max())
    # zlse = beta*(d-dmax); stable sum
    zlse = np.clip(beta*(d-dmax), -50, 50)  # since only relative to max; if beta*diff can be >50 underflow fine. Sum_e = ∑ exp(d_i-dmax)*beta. number terms large; for beta small, max diff? Use clip -500? If beta small, values around 0, sum_e ~m; if clip at -50 for below, ok? If beta=1000, diff maybe -1 -> -1000 clip -500 underflows but negligible. Use -700? exp(-700) underflow. But sum_e maybe m if beta small; diff all small. For beta large, only top few matter; clip -50 enough? e^-50=1.9e-22, negligible, but if many (m~e5), contribution e-17, negligible. Use -50 to avoid under/overflow. Positive clipped by 50 due d-dmax<=0, no positive.
    e = np.exp(zlse)
    sum_e = e.sum()
    if sum_e <=0: fallback uniform
    w = e/sum_e
    val = 2.0*(dmax + (np.log(sum_e) - np.log(m))/beta)
    C = float(np.dot(w, c))
    R = _shifted_convolution(a, w)
    # grad_all = 4*n*(a*(R-C))
    grad_vars = 4.0*n*(a[vars_idx]*(R[vars_idx]-C))
    grad_vars = nan_to_num
    return val, grad_vars
```
Check value for beta small: val approx 2*mean(d) + 2*(log sum - log m)/beta? Wait as beta→0, sum_e≈m, log term 0 => val=2*dmax, not average. That's wrong because expansion of LSE without subtract log m? Let's calculate: Standard LSE approx of max: (1/β) log( (1/m)∑ e^{β d_i}) = dmax + (1/β) log( (1/m)∑e^{β(d_i-dmax)}). At beta→0, ∑e≈m, log(1/m*m)=0, val≈2*dmax. That's not average, just max! Standard LSE with β→0 does NOT go to average; it goes to max? Wait logsumexp as β→0: (1/β) log((1/m)∑ e^{β d_i}) ≈ (1/β) log(1 + β mean(d - dmax)? Actually ∑ e^{β d_i} ≈ m + β∑d_i; (1/m)∑ ≈1+β(mean -dmax); log ≈β(mean-dmax); /β => mean-dmax; add dmax => mean. Yes if we use d_i not centered? Let's compute with centered: (1/β)log((1/m)∑e^{β(d_i-dmax)}) ≈ (1/β) log(1 + β(mean(d)-dmax))? e^{β(d-dmax)}≈1+β(d-dmax); average=1+β(mean-dmax); log≈β(mean-dmax). Add dmax => mean. So val should be 2*(dmax + (log(sum_e/m))/beta), where sum_e=∑e^{β(d-dmax)}. We subtract log m. As beta→0, log(sum_e/m)≈β(mean-dmax), finite mean. Good. For small beta, sum_e/m =1+ small; log stable. Our formula: val=2*(dmax + (log(sum_e) - log(m))/beta). Yes correct. It approaches 2*mean(d), not max, as beta small. Good. For beta large, log(sum_e/m) ≈ log(sum top e /m) negative large, wait if top e=1, m large, log(1/m)/β negative small, val≈2dmax. Good. Great.

But for beta=1000 and m=20000, if only top one e=1, val=2*dmax + negative 9.9/1000 ~0.0099 lower than max. Smooth max is below true max by ~log m/beta. Value not an upper bound but okay. The true exact max used for tracking. Gradient still soft.

Clip zlse negative to -50: for beta large, e non-top underflow; sum_e maybe top count. If all d equal, diff=0, e=1 all, sum_e=m. log(sum_e/m)=0. If below by small diff such that beta*diff < -50, ignored. Good. Positive none.

`m = 2*n -1`.

R shift convolution: `w` is length 2n-1. For `R_i = ∑ a_j w_{i+j}`, we use a_rev conv w. Need verify index with anchor? no. Implement `_shifted_convolution(a, w)`:
```
n=len(a); M=3*n-2; N=next_pow2(M)
AR=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
WR=np.fft.rfft(w, N)
out=np.fft.irfft(AR*WR, N)[:M]
return out[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Test with n=2, a=[a0,a1], w delta at k=1 (w[1]=1). a_rev=[a1,a0]. conv full length 3: index0 a1*w0=0,1 a1*w1 + a0*w0=a1,2 a0*w1+a1*w? =a0. M=3, out[1:3]=[a1,a0]. Good.

Shifted convolution with weighted multiple lags gives R.

Now _lbfgs_log_optimize detailed:
```
def _lbfgs_log_optimize(a0, deadline, label, betas=None, max_beta=None):
    a=_normalize(a0); n=a.size; best_a=a.copy(); best_score=_exact_score(a)
    if betas is None: betas=[200,1000,5000,20000,100000,500000,2000000,8000000]
    # time estimate by one eval
    anchor=int(np.argmax(a)); vars_idx=np.arange(n); vars_idx=...; z=_init_z(a, anchor, vars_idx)
    t0=time.monotonic(); val,grad=_lse_value_grad_log(z, betas[0], anchor, vars_idx)
    per=max(0.001, (time.monotonic()-t0)/max(1,1))
    total_budget = max(30, float(deadline - time.monotonic() - 30))
    n_beta = len(betas)
    per_beta_time = total_budget * 0.85 / n_beta
    for i,beta in enumerate(betas):
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic() - 10
        if rem < 20: break
        # allocate time = min(per_beta_time, rem/remaining_betas*?)
        segmented = min(per_beta_time, (deadline - time.monotonic() - 10 - (n_beta-i-1)*0) / 1) # not
```
Need fair allocation. Compute remaining time and number of remaining betas; time_per = (deadline - now - final_reserve) / (#remaining betas + maybe polish). maxfun=int(time_per/per). But L-BFGS maxfun cannot exceed maybe 3000. Use at least 30. 
```
        final_reserve = 10
        rem_total = max(0, deadline - time.monotonic() - final_reserve)
        time_per = rem_total / (n_beta - i)
        maxfun = int(max(30, min(5000, (time_per*0.85)/per)))
```

def _polish_active(a0, deadline, label='', k_list=(1,2,4,8,16), tol=1e-7, max_step=0.1):
    a=_normalize(a0); n=a.size; best=a.copy(); best_sc=_exact_score(a)
    v=np.zeros(n); t=1.0; no_improve=0; step_scale=1.0
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       it+=1
       # choose k cyclic
       k = k_list[it % len(k_list)]
       c=_fast_self_conv(a); d=n*c; dmax=float(d.max())
       # active: top k among d > dmax - max(1e-12, tol*dmax) maybe include top k regardless
       active = np.argsort(d, kind='stable')[-k:]
       active = active[np.argsort(d[active])]  # not needed
       # weights: uniform or softmax by differences
       diff = d[active] - dmax
       w_act = np.exp(np.clip(diff*1000, -50,0)); w_act/=w_act.sum()  # Since diff <=0; if dmax, top weight high.
       w_full=np.zeros(2*n-1); w_full[active]=w_act
       R=_shifted_convolution(a, w_full)
       C=float(np.dot(w_act, d[active])/n) # weighted c? d[active]=n c, so C_c = sum w c = (n sum w c)/n? Actually dot w_act*d[active] = n C_c. C = dot / n.
       g = R - C  # gradient direction without scale
       d = g - np.mean(g)  # remove constant for simplex? Maybe use.
       dnorm = np.linalg.norm(d)
       anorm = np.linalg.norm(a)
       if dnorm < 1e-15: break
       # Nesterov momentum? Use v
       t_new = (1+sqrt(1+4*t*t))/2; beta_m = (t-1)/t_new
       y = a + beta_m*v  # v previous step direction
       # normalize y for gradient? compute at y? To avoid extra c, maybe use a. Simpler momentum without Nesterov.
```

Build X:
```
def _make_fourier_X(n, K, rng, include_linear=True):
    x = np.linspace(0,1,n)
    cols=[]
    if include_linear: cols.append(1-2*x)
    for f in range(1,K+1):
       ph = 2*np.pi*f*x
       cols.append(np.cos(ph + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi) if f>2 else 0))
       cols.append(np.sin(ph + ...))
    X=np.column_stack(cols)
    # scale columns to unit norm to avoid ill-conditioning
    for j: X[:,j] /= norm
    return X
```

Top-level imports:

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize
_STOP_DEADLINE = 0.0

Helper _next_pow2.

_fast_self_conv:
```
def _fast_self_conv(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.empty(0)
    M=2*n-1
    N=_next_pow2(M)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    out=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:M]
    out=np.maximum(out,0.0)
    return out
```
`irfft` returns length N; if A*A has small imaginary? fine.

_shifted_convolution(a,w) as above. If w all zeros, return zeros. Check lengths: w length 2n-1.
```
def _shifted_convolution(a,w):
    n=len(a); M=3*n-2; N=_next_pow2(M)
    AR=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
    WR=np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(AR*WR,N)[:M]
    return conv[n-1:2*n-1]
```

_normalize.

_exact_score(unnormalized):
```
def _exact_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(...).ravel()
    n=a.size
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S <1e-12: return np.inf
    c=_fast_self_conv(a)
    mx=float(c.max())
    if mx <=0: return np.inf
    return float(2.0*n*mx/(S*S))
```

_resample:
```
def _resample(a,n):
    a=...; m=a.size
    if m==n: return a.copy()
    if m==0: return np.ones(n)/n
    old=np.linspace(0,1,m); new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    out=np.interp(new,old,a)
    return _normalize(out)
```

_generate_inits(n,rng,parent,cap=50):
Need avoid closure? We can define helper top-level `_add_init(out, arr, cap)`.
```
def _add_init(out, arr, cap):
   if len(out)>=cap: return
   arr=np.asarray(arr,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   arr=np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
   arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,None)
   if arr.size != len(out[0])? We don't know n. Use size !=? just if arr.size==0
   S=arr.sum(); if S<1e-12 return
   arr=arr/S
   # optional skip if too close to existing? maybe use squared distance
   for b in out:
      if np.linalg.norm(arr-b) < 1e-4: return
   out.append(arr)
```
The pairwise norm O(cap^2) okay.

for _ in range(6):
  K=12; z=np.zeros(n)
  for f in range(1,K+1):
    z += (1/f**0.6)*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
  _add_init(out,np.exp(0.5*(z - z.mean())))

for _ in range(4):
  noise=rng.standard_normal(n); width=int(rng.integers(3, max(4,n//50+3))); if width>1: kern=np.ones(width)/width; noise=np.convolve(noise,kern,mode='same'); _add_init(out,np.exp(0.5*(noise-noise.mean())))
```

```
def _make_fourier_X(n, K, rng, include_linear=True):
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    cols=[]
    if include_linear:
       cols.append(1.0-2.0*x)
    cols.append(1.0)?? If include constant, gradient null. Maybe include constant? It allows mean of log density; but scale invariance means constant cancels. Including constant column leads flat direction; L-BFGS may waste. Omit.
    for f in range(1,K+1):
       phase_c = 0.0 if f<=2 else rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       phase_s = 0.0 if f<=2 else rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       cols.append(np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x + phase_c))
       cols.append(np.sin(2*np.pi*f*x + phase_s))
    X=np.column_stack(cols)
    # scale columns
    for j in range(X.shape[1]):
       nrm=np.linalg.norm(X[:,j])
       if nrm>1e-12: X[:,j]/=nrm
    return X
```

def _new_anchor_vars(a):

   anchor=int(np.argmax(a))
   vars_idx=np.array([i for i in range(n) if i != anchor], dtype=np.int64)  # list comprehension okay no lambda.
   return anchor, vars_idx

`_lbfgs_log_optimize` code:
```
def _lbfgs_log_optimize(a0, deadline, label, betas=None):
    global _STOP_DEADLINE
    a=_normalize(a0); n=a.size
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=_exact_score(a)
    if betas is None:
        betas=[100,500,2000,10000,50000,200000,1000000,4000000]
    anchor, vars = _new_anchor_vars(a)
    z=_init_z_from_a(a,anchor,vars)
    # time estimate
    t_est=time.monotonic(); val,g=_lse_value_grad_log(z,betas[0],anchor,vars); per=max(1e-4,(time.monotonic()-t_est))
    n_beta=len(betas)
    for bi,beta in enumerate(betas):
        now=time.monotonic()
        rem=deadline - now - 5.0
        if rem < 15: break
        rem_total=deadline - now - 5.0
        time_per=rem_total/(n_beta-bi)
        maxfun=int(max(30, min(4000, (time_per*0.85)/per)))
        # Optionally if per small, maxfun high.
        _STOP_DEADLINE = now + min(time_per*0.95, rem*0.95) - 1.0
        try:
            res=minimize(_lse_value_grad_log, z, args=(beta,anchor,vars), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, callback=_callback_deadline, options={'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-10,'maxiter':max(30,maxfun//2),'maxfun':maxfun,'maxls':10})
            if res.x is not None: z=res.x
        except Exception:
            pass
        a2=_z_to_a(z,anchor,vars)
        sc2=_exact_score(a2)
        if finite and sc2<best_sc: best_sc=sc2; best_a=a2.copy()
        # re-anchor
        a=_normalize(a2)
        anchor, vars = _new_anchor_vars(a)
        z=_init_z_from_a(a,anchor,vars)
        # maybe if score not improved after high beta and time left, break? continue.
    # after, try reverse? no caller.
    return best_sc, best_a
```
Note: _STOP_DEADLINE set to now + time_per*0.95 maybe if time_per > rem? Use min. Callback stop if time > _STOP_DEADLINE. Good. But if L-BFGS maxfun time long and callback only after iterations, may overrun but okay.

For final high betas, `betas` can start high. If previous z from previous optimize with different anchor, we re-init. Good.

Potential issue: L-BFGS with high beta and z initialized from current a; value gradient maybe zero if at local optimum; it returns. Fine.

Now `_polish_exact`: Let's implement minimal but robust. Use `_pair_score_key`.
```
def _polish_exact(a0, deadline, label=''):
    a=_normalize(a0); n=a.size
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=_exact_score(a); cur_sc=best_sc
    k_cycle=[1,2,4,8,4,2,1]
    it=0; no_improve=0; base_mult=1.0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 5:
       it+=1
       k = k_cycle[it % len(k_cycle)]
       c=_fast_self_conv(a); d=n*c; dmax=float(d.max())
       # top indices
       active = np.argsort(d, kind='stable')[-k:]
       active = active[np.argsort(d[active])]  # maybe not needed, argsort returns ascending by value? If we take (-k:) from sorted ascending, they are in ascending order. fine.
       # weights softmax based on d differences (dmax)
       diff = d[active] - dmax
       # If only top value is dmax, diff for top 0. Use beta=2000? Since diff maybe small ~1e-4. To spread near peaks, use 200? We'll use beta_w = 500.
       wact=np.exp(np.clip(diff*500.0, -50.0, 0.0)); sw=wact.sum(); if sw<=0: wact=np.ones(k)/k else wact/=sw
       wfull=np.zeros(2*n-1); wfull[active]=wact
       R=_shifted_convolution(a,wfull)
       C=float(np.dot(wact, d[active]))/n  # weighted c
       g=R-C
       # remove constant to keep sum? maybe g -= np.mean(g)
       g = g - np.mean(g)
       gnorm=np.linalg.norm(g)
       if gnorm < 1e-15: break
       anorm=np.linalg.norm(a)
       step=0.05*anorm/ (gnorm*base_mult? Actually base_mult scales stop)
       best_cand=a; best_cand_sc=cur_sc
       improved=False
       for mult in (1.0,0.5,0.25):
           x=a - (step*mult)*g
           x=np.clip(x,0.0,None); S=x.sum()
           if S<1e-12: continue
           x /= S
           sc=_exact_score(x)
           if sc < best_cand_sc + 1e-12:
               best_cand=x; best_cand_sc=sc
               if sc < cur_sc - 1e-12: improved=True
               if sc < best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_a=x.copy()
       a=best_cand; cur_sc=best_cand_sc
       if improved:
           no_improve=0
       else:
           no_improve +=1
           if no_improve>15:
               base_mult *=0.5
               if base_mult<1e-3: break
               no_improve=0
               # perturbation
               if it % 30 ==0:
                  rng = np.random.default_rng(it)
                  noise = rng.standard_normal(n)*1e-3
                  a=_normalize(np.clip(a+noise,0,None))
       # Time check
    return best_sc,best_a
```
Step calculation: `step = 0.05 * anorm / (gnorm * base_mult)`? If base_mult reduces, step larger? We want base_mult reduce step? Actually mult loop already. To reduce step when no improve, multiply base factor: `step = (0.05*anorm/gnorm)*base_mult`, with base_mult starts1 and reduces. Good.

But if base_mult<1 step smaller. If no improve for long, break. This might not monotonic; but line search ensures candidate no worse than current? We pick best_cand_sc initialized cur_sc; if none better, best_cand remains a. If equal, a unchanged. Good.

Could be too slow: each iter does 1 self conv (c), 1 shifted FFT, and up to3 exact scores (each self conv). 4 FFTs per iter. For 32768 ~0.4s, 200 iters=80s. Fine. Use final 100s maybe 200 iters. Good.

But active weights top k=16 with wfull full and FFT; okay. If k large, top arg sort O(n log n) each iter; for n=32768, fine. Could use `np.argpartition` for k small. Simpler argsort.

We need avoid lambda. In `_polish_exact`, use `active = np.argsort(d, kind='stable')[-k:]`. This returns indices of k largest in ascending order among them. Good.

def _cleanup_best(a, best_sc):
   a=_normalize(a); best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=_exact_score(a)
   rev=a[::-1]; sc=_exact_score(rev); if sc<best_sc: best_a=rev; best_sc=sc
   # zero thresholds
   maxv=best_a.max()
   for tau in [0,1e-12,1e-11,1e-10,1e-9,1e-8,1e-7,5e-7,1e-6,5e-6,1e-5]:
       b=best_a.copy()
       if tau>0: b[b < tau*maxv] = 0.0
       b=_normalize(b)
       sc=_exact_score(b)
       if sc < best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_a=b
       # also reverse of zeroed?
       rv=b[::-1]; sc2=_exact_score(rv); if sc2<best_sc: ...
   return best_sc,best_a
```

`_lowdim_shape_search` chunking:
```
def _lowdim_shape_search(n, deadline, rng, parent):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    if parent is not None:
       best_a=_resample(parent,n); best_sc=_exact_score(best_a)
    else:
       best_a=np.ones(n)/n; best_sc=2.0
    rem=deadline-t0
    num_trials=max(1, int(rem/40))
    trials=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline-30 and num_trials>0:
       K=[5,8,12,16,10,20][trials%6]
       X=_make_fourier_X(n,K,rng)
       c0 = rng.normal(0,0.5,X.shape[1]) if trials>0 else np.zeros(X.shape[1])
       # sub_dead evenly
       sub_dead = min(deadline-20, t0 + (trials+1)*rem/num_trials)
       sc,a=_lowdim_shape_optimize(X,c0,sub_dead, label='fd{}'.format(trials))
       if sc<best: update
       trials +=1
       num_trials -=1
    return best_a
```
But `rem` computed once; sub_dead uses t0 + (trial+1)*chunk. Good. If parent exists, lowshape initialized parent resample. If no parent, uniform.

Now let's scrutinize mathematical objective and gradient in log L-BFGS with LSE weights. Let's verify with a simple n=2 example maybe. a=(0.5,0.5), n=2, c=[0.25,0.5,0.25], d=n c=[0.5,1,0.5]. Actually n=2 optimum score2 uniform. Good.

For n=5 example, if sequence [0.4,0.2,0,0.2,0.2] score1.6, gradient maybe not zero due positive approximations.

Now check gradient of LSE value wrt z with anchor. We used grad_z=4n a(R-C). Let's rederive for anchor p0=1. Objective F=2L, L=(1/β)log((1/m)∑exp(β d)). d_k=n c_k(a). For variable z_i (i not anchor): da_j/dz_i = a_i(δ_{ij}-p_anchor? Wait p_sheet? In log-linear with anchor fixed, p_i=e^{z_i}, p_anchor=1, S=∑p. da_j = (δ_{ij}p_i - p_j a_i? Actually a_j=p_j/S. ∂a_j/∂z_i = δ_{ij}p_i/S - p_j p_i/S^2 = a_i(δ_{ij}-a_j). Same as softmax all variables? For anchor variable excluded, but for j=anchor, a_anchor =1/S; ∂a_anchor/∂z_i = -p_i/S^2 = -a_i a_anchor = a_i(0 - a_anchor). Thus formula ∂a_j = a_i(δ_{ij}-a_j) holds for all j including anchor, with δ_{ij}=0. Good. Then ∂c_k = ∑_j 2a_{k-j} ∂a_j =2a_i a_{k-i} -2a_i ∑_j a_j a_{k-j}=2a_i(a_{k-i}-c_k). Weighted ∂F=2∑w n * 2a_i(a_{k-i}-c_k)=4n a_i(R_i-C). Perfect. No missing scale. Great.

For full softmax variable all, same formula, sum zero. For anchor, gradient over omitted variables not sum zero; good.

Now value: use d=n*c. Since a sum1. Good.

One subtle point: LSE approximation: val = 2*(dmax + (log(sum_e)-log(m))/beta), with sum_e = ∑exp(β(d-dmax)). Derivative of val wrt a: 2∑w ∂d, w=e/sum_e (no 1/m because derivative of log(sum_e/m)=derivative log sum_e). Good.

Sum_e maybe less than m for large beta, but that's intended (weights top). For beta=100, diff for second peak maybe -0.001, z=-0.1, included. Good. If beta=1e4, diff -1e-4 => -1 included. Fine. Clip -50 ignores diff < -50/beta. Good.

Potential issue: If beta very small, val formula with sum_e/m: sum_e ~m, log term ~β(mean-dmax)/β = mean-dmax, val=2mean(d). Good. But if beta=100 and d range ~1, okay.

def _lowdim_shape_optimize(X, c0, deadline, label=''):
    global _STOP_DEADLINE
    n=X.shape[0]
    c0=np.asarray(c0,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    # initial a/score
    z=X.dot(c0); ... a... sc
    t_est=time; val,g=_lowdim_lse_value_grad(c0,X,betas[0]); per=max(1e-4, elapsed)
    betas=[50,200,1000,5000,20000,100000,500000]
    n_beta=len(betas)
    for bi,beta in enumerate(betas):
       now=time; rem=deadline-now-2; if rem<10 break
       time_per=(deadline-now-2)/(n_beta-bi)
       maxfun=int(max(20,min(3000,(time_per*0.85)/per)))
       _STOP_DEADLINE=now+min(time_per*0.98, rem*0.98)-0.5
       try: res=minimize(... args=(X,beta) ...)
       except: pass
       c=res.x; a=softmax; sc; update
    return best_sc,best_a
```
Need `_softmax_a(z)` helper? Define top-level `_softmax_a(z)` to avoid duplicate. 
```
def _softmax_a(z):
    z=np.asarray(z,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if z.size==0: return np.array([])
    maxz=float(z.max())
    p=np.exp(z-maxz)
    S=float(p.sum())
    if S<=0 or not finite: return np.ones(z.size)/z.size
    return p/S
```
Use in lowdim and maybe cleanup.

def _lp_improve(a0, deadline, n_target=2048, max_iters=6, label=''):
    global? no
    a=_resample(a0, n_target)
    best=a; best_sc=_exact_score(a)
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5: break
       n=a.size; c=_fast_self_conv(a); T=float(c.max())
       # T on normalized? a sum1.
       # If T==0 skip
       G=np.empty((2*n-1,n), dtype=np.float64)
       # build
       for k in range(2*n-1): ...
       try:
          from scipy.optimize import linprog? We can import at top `from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog`
          res=linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=G, b_ub=T*np.ones(2*n-1), bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
       except Exception:
          break
       if res.status !=0: break
       S=float(np.sum(res.x))
       if not finite or S <= 1.0005: break
       g=res.x/S
       # line search update
       improved=False
       for alpha in (0.3,0.5,0.7,1.0):
          x=(1-alpha)*a + alpha*g; x=np.clip(x,0,None); x=_normalize(x); sc=_exact_score(x)
          if sc < best_sc:
             best_sc=sc; best=x.copy(); improved=True; break
          if sc < _exact_score(a):? not track.
       a=best.copy() if improved? If no improve, break maybe; or try smaller alpha? Add 0.1.
       if not improved:
          break
    return best_sc,best

Let's write `_lp_improve` more carefully with deadline.
```
def _lp_improve(a0, deadline, n_target=2048, max_rows=512, max_iters=8, label=''):
    if linprog is None: return _exact_score(a0), _normalize(a0)
    a=_resample(a0,n_target); best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=_exact_score(a)
    n=a.size
    for it in range(max_iters):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 10: break
        c=_fast_self_conv(a); T=float(c.max())
        if not finite or T<=0: break
        r=max(1, min(max_rows, c.size))
        active = np.argsort(c, kind='stable')[-r:]
        G=np.empty((r,n), dtype=np.float64)
        for row_idx,k in enumerate(active):
            lo=max(0, int(k)- (n-1)); hi=min(n-1, int(k))
            G[row_idx,:]=0
            if hi>=lo:
                G[row_idx,lo:hi+1] = a[int(k)-hi : int(k)-lo+1][::-1]
        b=np.full(r, T, dtype=np.float64)
        c_obj=-np.ones(n)
        bounds=[(0.0,None)] for _ in range(n)? Can't use lambda? list comprehension okay? No lambda: `bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n` works (list of same tuple immutable okay). 
        t_lp=time.monotonic()
        try:
            res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=G, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
        except Exception:
            break
        if res is None or res.status !=0: break
        x=res.x
        S=float(np.sum(x))
        if not finite or S <= 1.0005: break
        g=x/S
        improved=False
        for alpha in (0.3,0.5,0.7,0.2,0.1,1.0):
            xx=(1-alpha)*a + alpha*g
            xx=np.clip(xx,0,None); xx=_normalize(xx)
            sc=_exact_score(xx)
            if sc < best_sc - 1e-10:
                best_sc=sc; best_a=xx.copy(); improved=True; break
            if sc < _exact_score(a) - 1e-10:
                # accept as current even if not global? but track
                pass
        if not improved:
            # try maybe g alone if much better? no
            break
        a=best_a.copy()
        print(...)
    return best_sc,best_a
```
The line `G[row_idx,:]=0` inside loop O(rn), then assign segment. Could optimize by creating row zeros each. okay for r=512,n=2048 ~1M.

def _softmax_a(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if z.size == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    maxz = float(np.max(z))
    p = np.exp(np.clip(z - maxz, -50.0, 0.0))
    S = float(np.sum(p))
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 0.0:
        return np.full(z.size, 1.0 / z.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return p / S
```

```
def _generate_inits(n, rng, parent, cap=40):
    out=[]
    if parent is not None:
        p=_resample(parent,n)
        _add_init(out,p,n,cap)
        _add_init(out,p[::-1],n,cap)
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    _add_init(out,np.ones(n),n,cap)
    # ramps
    for amp in (0.1,0.3,0.6,1.0,1.5,2.0):
       _add_init(out,1.0+amp*(1.0-2.0*x),n,cap)
       _add_init(out,1.0+amp*(2.0*x-1.0),n,cap)
    # V/inverted
    for amp in (0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0):
       _add_init(out,1.0+amp*np.abs(2.0*x-1.0),n,cap)
       _add_init(out,1.0+amp*(1.0-np.abs(2.0*x-1.0)**2),n,cap)
    # two blocks
    for Lf in (0.5,0.6,0.65,0.7,0.75,0.8,0.85,0.9):
       l1=int(max(1,min(n-1,round(Lf*n))))
       l2=n-l1
       for pf in (0.3,0.4,0.5,0.6,0.7,0.8):
          arr=np.empty(n)
          arr[:l1]=pf/l1
          arr[l1:]=(1.0-pf)/l2
          for w in (0.02,0.05,0.1):
             width=int(min(512,max(3,int(round(w*n)))))
             if width>1:
                kern=np.ones(width)/width
                sm=np.convolve(arr,kern,mode='same')
             else: sm=arr.copy()
             _add_init(out,sm,n,cap)
             if len(out)>=cap: break
          if len(out)>=cap: break
       if len(out)>=cap: break
    # center bumps
    for ctr in (0.25,0.35,0.5,0.65,0.75):
       for w in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4):
          _add_init(out,1.0+1.0*np.exp(-((x-ctr)/w)**2),n,cap)
          if len(out)>=cap: break
    # low-freq random
    for _ in range(6):
       z=np.zeros(n)
       for f in range(1,13):
          z += (1.0/(f**0.6))*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*f*x + rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*np.pi))
       _add_init(out,np.exp(0.5*(z-z.mean())),n,cap)
    # random smooth noise
    for _ in range(4):
       noise=rng.standard_normal(n)
       width=int(rng.integers(3, max(4,min(512,n//20+3))))
       if width>1:
          kern=np.ones(width)/width
          noise=np.convolve(noise,kern,mode='same')
       _add_init(out,np.exp(0.5*(noise-noise.mean())),n,cap)
    if not out:
       out.append(np.ones(n)/n)
    return out
```

def _add_init(out, arr, expected_size, cap):
    if len(out) >= cap: return
    arr=np.asarray(arr,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size != expected_size: return
    arr=np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,None)
    S=float(arr.sum())
    if S <= 1e-12: return
    arr=arr/S
    out.append(arr)

```python
"""
Minimize 2*n*max(conv(a,a))/sum(a)^2 for non-negative a.

Strategy
--------
1. Low-dimensional Fourier-coordinate optimization finds a good global shape.
2. A small dense active-set LP direction (max 1^T g s.t. top active
   cross-convolution constraints) is applied at low resolution to sharpen the
   shape.
3. Full-resimum(log-simplex) L-LSE optimization using L-BFGS refines the
   sequence.  The parameterization fixes one coefficient as an anchor, removes
   the scale invariance, and can be re-anchored between beta stages.
4. Multiple lengths are explored.  Global best is polished with exact max
   active-set projected gradient, thresholding, and reversal.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog
except Exception:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
    linprog = None

_STOP_DEADLINE = 0.0


def _callback_deadline(*args):
    return time.monotonic() > _STOP_DEADLINE


def _pair_key(item):
    return item[0]


def _triple_key(item):
    return item[0]


def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _fast_self_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    M = 2 * n - 1
    N = _next_pow2(M)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    out = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:M]
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    return out


def _shifted_convolution(a, w):
    """
    Return R_i = sum_j a_j w_{i+j}, where w has length 2n-1.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    M = 3 * n - 2
    N = _next_pow2(M)
    AR = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
    WR = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(AR * WR, N)[:M]
    return conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-15:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, a.size)
    return a / S


def _exact_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    S = float(a.sum())
    if n == 0 or S < 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    c = _fast_self_conv(a)
    mx = float(c.max())
    if mx <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(mx):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * mx / (S * S))


def _resample(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(n)
    m = a.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    if m == n:
        return _normalize(a)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return _normalize(out)


def _softmax_a(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if z.size == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        return np.full(z.size, 1.0 / z.size, dtype=np.float64)
    maxz = float(np.max(z))
    p = np.exp(np.clip(z - maxz, -50.0, 0.0))
    S = float(np.sum(p))
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 0.0:
        return np.full(z.size, 1.0 / z.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return p / S


def _add_init(out, arr, expected_size, cap):
    if len(out) >= cap:
        return
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size != expected_size:
        return
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, None)
    S = float(arr.sum())
    if S < 1e-12:
        return
    arr = arr / S
    out.append(arr)


def _generate_inits(n, rng, parent, cap=40):
    out = []

    if parent is not None:
        p = _resample(parent, n)
        _add_init(out, p, n, cap)
        _add_init(out, p[::-1], n, cap)

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    _add_init(out, np.ones(n), n, cap)

    for amp in (0.1, 0.3, 0.6, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0):
        _add_init(out, 1.0 + amp * (1.0 - 2.0 * x), n, cap)
        _add_init(out, 1.0 + amp * (2.0 * x - 1.0), n, cap)

    for amp in (0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        _add_init(out, 1.0 + amp * np.abs(2.0 * x - 1.0), n, cap)
        _add_init(out, 1.0 + amp * (1.0 - np.abs(2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2), n, cap)

    # Asymmetric two-block step functions with optional smoothing.
    for Lf in (0.5, 0.6, 0.65, 0.7, 0.75, 0.8, 0.85, 0.9):
        l1 = int(max(1, min(n - 1, round(Lf * n))))
        l2 = n - l1
        if l2 < 1:
            continue
        for pf in (0.3, 0.4, 0.5, 0.6, 0.7, 0.8):
            base = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
            base[:l1] = pf / l1
            base[l1:] = (1.0 - pf) / l2
            for w in (0.0, 0.02, 0.05, 0.1):
                arr = base.copy()
                if w > 0.0:
                    width = int(min(512, max(3, int(round(w * n)))))
                    if width > 1:
                        kern = np.ones(width) / width
                        arr = np.convolve(arr, kern, mode="same")
                _add_init(out, arr, n, cap)
                if len(out) >= cap:
                    break
            if len(out) >= cap:
                break
        if len(out) >= cap:
            break

    # Asymmetric center bumps.
    for ctr in (0.25, 0.35, 0.5, 0.65, 0.75):
        for w in (0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.4):
            _add_init(out, 1.0 + np.exp(-((x - ctr) / w) ** 2), n, cap)
            if len(out) >= cap:
                break
        if len(out) >= cap:
            break

    # Low-frequency Fourier-like random smooth shapes.
    for _ in range(6):
        z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for f in range(1, 13):
            z += (1.0 / (f ** 0.6)) * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        _add_init(out, np.exp(0.5 * (z - z.mean())), n, cap)

    # Smooth random noise.
    for _ in range(4):
        noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
        width = int(rng.integers(3, max(4, min(512, n // 20 + 3))))
        if width > 1:
            kern = np.ones(width) / width
            noise = np.convolve(noise, kern, mode="same")
        _add_init(out, np.exp(0.5 * (noise - noise.mean())), n, cap)

    if not out:
        out.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n)

    return out


def _quick_top_inits(n, parent, rng, topk=3, cap=30):
    arrs = _generate_inits(n, rng, parent, cap=cap)
    scored = []
    for arr in arrs:
        s = _exact_score(arr)
        if np.isfinite(s):
            scored.append((s, arr))
    scored.sort(key=_pair_key)
    return [arr for _, arr in scored[:topk]]


def _make_fourier_X(n, K, rng, include_linear=True):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    cols = []
    if include_linear:
        cols.append(1.0 - 2.0 * x)
    for f in range(1, K + 1):
        ph_c = 0.0 if f <= 2 else rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        ph_s = 0.0 if f <= 2 else rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        cols.append(np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + ph_c))
        cols.append(np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + ph_s))
    X = np.column_stack(cols).astype(np.float64)
    if X.size == 0:
        return X
    for j in range(X.shape[1]):
        nrm = float(np.linalg.norm(X[:, j]))
        if nrm > 1e-14:
            X[:, j] /= nrm
    return X


def _lowdim_lse_value_grad(coef, X, beta):
    coef = np.asarray(coef, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = coef.size
    if m == 0:
        return 1e100, np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(coef)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)

    z = X.dot(coef)
    a = _softmax_a(z)
    n = a.size

    c = _fast_self_conv(a)
    d = n * c
    mconv = c.size
    dmax = float(d.max())
    if not np.isfinite(dmax):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)

    y = np.clip(beta * (d - dmax), -50.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(y)
    se = float(np.sum(e))
    if se <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(se):
        e = np.ones(mconv, dtype=np.float64)
        se = float(mconv)
    w = e / se

    val = 2.0 * (dmax + (float(np.log(se)) - float(np.log(mconv))) / beta)
    C = float(np.dot(w, c))
    R = _shifted_convolution(a, w)
    grad_z = 4.0 * n * a * (R - C)
    grad_z = np.nan_to_num(grad_z, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

    grad = X.T.dot(grad_z)
    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return float(val), grad


def _lowdim_shape_optimize(X, coef0, deadline, label=""):
    global _STOP_DEADLINE
    coef0 = np.asarray(coef0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if coef0.size == 0:
        return np.inf, np.ones(X.shape[0], dtype=np.float64) / X.shape[0]

    n = X.shape[0]
    a = _softmax_a(X.dot(coef0))
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _exact_score(a)

    betas = (50.0, 500.0, 5000.0, 50000.0, 300000.0)
    nb = len(betas)

    tt = time.monotonic()
    try:
        _lowdim_lse_value_grad(coef0, X, betas[0])
        per = max(1e-4, time.monotonic() - tt)
    except Exception:
        per = 0.01

    coef = coef0.copy()
    for bi, beta in enumerate(betas):
        now = time.monotonic()
        rem = deadline - now - 2.0
        if rem < 5.0:
            break
        time_per = rem / max(1, nb - bi)
        maxfun = int(max(20, min(5000, (time_per * 0.85) / max(per, 1e-4))))
        _STOP_DEADLINE = now + min(time_per * 0.97, rem * 0.97) - 0.2

        try:
            res = minimize(
                _lowdim_lse_value_grad,
                coef,
                args=(X, beta),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                callback=_callback_deadline,
                options={
                    "maxcor": 10,
                    "maxiter": max(30, maxfun // 2),
                    "maxfun": maxfun,
                    "ftol": 1e-16,
                    "gtol": 1e-10,
                    "maxls": 10,
                },
            )
            if res.x is not None:
                coef = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        except Exception:
            pass

        a2 = _softmax_a(X.dot(coef))
        sc2 = _exact_score(a2)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc2
            best_a = a2.copy()
        else:
            # Keep the optimizer on the best known point if this stage was bad.
            coef = coef.copy()

    return best_sc, best_a


def _lowdim_shape_search(n, deadline, rng, parent):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    inits = _quick_top_inits(n, parent, rng, topk=3, cap=25)

    best_a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    best_sc = _exact_score(best_a)

    for arr in inits:
        sc = _exact_score(arr)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = arr.copy()

    max_trials = int(max(1, min(10, (deadline - t0) / 45.0)))
    trial = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0 and trial < max_trials:
        K = (5, 8, 10, 12, 16, 20)[trial % 6]
        X = _make_fourier_X(n, K, rng)
        if trial == 0:
            coef0 = np.zeros(X.shape[1], dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            coef0 = rng.normal(0.0, 0.5, size=X.shape[1])
        sub_dead = min(deadline - 60.0, time.monotonic() + 50.0)
        sc, a = _lowdim_shape_optimize(X, coef0, sub_dead, "fd{}".format(trial))
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()
            print("lowdim fd{}: {:.8f}".format(trial, sc))
        trial += 1

    return best_a, best_sc


def _lp_improve(a0, deadline, n_target=2048, max_rows=512, max_iters=8, label=""):
    if linprog is None:
        return _exact_score(a0), _normalize(a0)

    a = _resample(a0, n_target)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _exact_score(a)
    n = a.size
    if n < 8:
        return best_sc, best_a

    for it in range(max_iters):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now > deadline - 15.0:
            break

        c = _fast_self_conv(a)
        T = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(T) or T <= 0.0:
            break

        r = int(max(1, min(max_rows, c.size)))
        active = np.argsort(c, kind="stable")[-r:]
        G = np.empty((r, n), dtype=np.float64)

        for row, k in enumerate(active):
            G[row, :] = 0.0
            ki = int(k)
            lo = max(0, ki - (n - 1))
            hi = min(n - 1, ki)
            if hi >= lo:
                G[row, lo:hi + 1] = a[ki - hi:ki - lo + 1][::-1]

        b = np.full(r, T, dtype=np.float64)
        c_obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n

        try:
            res = linprog(
                c_obj,
                A_ub=G,
                b_ub=b,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
                options={"presolve": True},
            )
        except Exception:
            break

        if res is None or int(getattr(res, "status", 1)) != 0:
            break

        x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        S = float(np.sum(x))
        if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 1.0005:
            break
        g = x / S

        old_sc = best_sc
        cur_a = a.copy()
        cur_sc = old_sc

        for alpha in (0.3, 0.5, 0.7, 0.2, 0.1, 1.0):
            xx = (1.0 - alpha) * cur_a + alpha * g
            xx = np.clip(xx, 0.0, None)
            xx = _normalize(xx)
            sc = _exact_score(xx)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur_sc:
                cur_a = xx.copy()
                cur_sc = sc
                if sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_a = xx.copy()

        if cur_sc < old_sc - 1e-12:
            a = cur_a.copy()
            if cur_sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = cur_sc
                best_a = a.copy()
            print("lp{} it{}: n={} score={:.8f}".format(label, it, n, best_sc))
        else:
            break

    return best_sc, best_a


def _new_anchor_vars(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return 0, np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64)
    anchor = int(np.argmax(a))
    if a[anchor] <= 0.0:
        anchor = n // 2
    vars_idx = np.array([i for i in range(n) if i != anchor], dtype=np.int64)
    return anchor, vars_idx


def _init_z_from_a(a, anchor, vars_idx):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    safe = np.clip(a, 1e-300, None)
    z = np.log(safe[vars_idx] / safe[anchor])
    return np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)


def _z_to_a(z, anchor, vars_idx):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = vars_idx.size + 1
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        a = np.full(n, 1.0 / max(1, n), dtype=np.float64)
        return a

    if z.size:
        maxz = max(0.0, float(np.max(z)))
    else:
        maxz = 0.0

    p = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p[anchor] = float(np.exp(np.clip(-maxz, -50.0, 0.0)))
    if z.size:
        p[vars_idx] = np.exp(np.clip(z - maxz, -50.0, 0.0))

    S = float(np.sum(p))
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return p / S


def _lse_value_grad_log(z, beta, anchor, vars_idx):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(vars_idx.size, dtype=np.float64)

    a = _z_to_a(z, anchor, vars_idx)
    n = a.size

    c = _fast_self_conv(a)
    d = n * c
    mconv = c.size
    dmax = float(d.max())
    if not np.isfinite(dmax):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(vars_idx.size, dtype=np.float64)

    y = np.clip(beta * (d - dmax), -50.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(y)
    se = float(np.sum(e))
    if se <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(se):
        e = np.ones(mconv, dtype=np.float64)
        se = float(mconv)
    w = e / se

    val = 2.0 * (dmax + (float(np.log(se)) - float(np.log(mconv))) / beta)
    C = float(np.dot(w, c))
    R = _shifted_convolution(a, w)
    grad_all = 4.0 * n * a * (R - C)
    grad_all = np.nan_to_num(grad_all, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

    grad = grad_all[vars_idx]
    return float(val), grad


def _lbfgs_log_optimize(a0, deadline, label="", betas=None):
    global _STOP_DEADLINE

    a = _normalize(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf, np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _exact_score(a)

    if betas is None:
        betas = (100.0, 500.0, 2000.0, 10000.0, 50000.0, 200000.0, 1000000.0, 4000000.0)
    betas = tuple(float(b) for b in betas)
    nb = len(betas)

    anchor, vars_idx = _new_anchor_vars(a)
    z = _init_z_from_a(a, anchor, vars_idx)

    tt = time.monotonic()
    try:
        _lse_value_grad_log(z, betas[0], anchor, vars_idx)
        per = max(1e-4, time.monotonic() - tt)
    except Exception:
        per = 0.02

    for bi, beta in enumerate(betas):
        now = time.monotonic()
        rem = deadline - now - 5.0
        if rem < 12.0:
            break

        time_per = rem / max(1, nb - bi)
        maxfun = int(max(30, min(8000, (time_per * 0.85) / max(per, 1e-4))))
        _STOP_DEADLINE = now + min(time_per * 0.98, rem * 0.98) - 1.0

        try:
            res = minimize(
                _lse_value_grad_log,
                z,
                args=(beta, anchor, vars_idx),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                callback=_callback_deadline,
                options={
                    "maxcor": 10,
                    "maxiter": max(30, maxfun // 2),
                    "maxfun": maxfun,
                    "ftol": 1e-16,
                    "gtol": 1e-10,
                    "maxls": 10,
                },
            )
            if res.x is not None:
                z = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        except Exception:
            pass

        a2 = _z_to_a(z, anchor, vars_idx)
        sc2 = _exact_score(a2)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc2
            best_a = a2.copy()
            print("{} beta={:.0f}: {:.8f}".format(label, beta, best_sc))

        a = _normalize(a2)
        anchor, vars_idx = _new_anchor_vars(a)
        z = _init_z_from_a(a, anchor, vars_idx)

    return best_sc, best_a


def _polish_exact(a0, deadline, label=""):
    a = _normalize(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf, a0

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _exact_score(a)
    cur_sc = best_sc

    k_cycle = (1, 2, 4, 8, 4, 2, 1)
    it = 0
    no_improve = 0
    base_mult = 1.0

    rng_seed = 123456
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 8.0:
        it += 1
        k = k_cycle[it % len(k_cycle)]

        c = _fast_self_conv(a)
        d = n * c
        dmax = float(d.max())
        if not np.isfinite(dmax):
            break

        active = np.argsort(d, kind="stable")[-k:]
        diff = d[active] - dmax
        wact = np.exp(np.clip(diff * 500.0, -50.0, 0.0))
        sw = float(wact.sum())
        if sw <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sw):
            wact = np.full(k, 1.0 / k, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            wact /= sw

        wfull = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        wfull[active] = wact

        R = _shifted_convolution(a, wfull)
        C = float(np.dot(wact, d[active])) / n
        g = R - C
        g = g - float(np.mean(g))
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
        anorm = float(np.linalg.norm(a))
        if gnorm < 1e-15:
            break

        step = (0.05 * anorm / gnorm) * base_mult

        best_cand = a.copy()
        best_cand_sc = cur_sc
        improved = False

        for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25):
            x = a - step * mult * g
            x = np.clip(x, 0.0, None)
            S = float(x.sum())
            if S < 1e-12:
                continue
            x /= S
            sc = _exact_score(x)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_cand_sc:
                best_cand = x.copy()
                best_cand_sc = sc
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur_sc - 1e-12:
                improved = True
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = x.copy()

        a = best_cand
        cur_sc = best_cand_sc

        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 15:
                base_mult *= 0.5
                if base_mult < 1e-3:
                    break
                no_improve = 0
                if it % 30 == 0:
                    rngp = np.random.default_rng(rng_seed + it)
                    scale = 1e-4 / max(1.0, np.sqrt(float(n)))
                    a = _normalize(np.clip(a + scale * rngp.standard_normal(n), 0.0, None))
                    cur_sc = _exact_score(a)

    return best_sc, best_a


def _cleanup_best(a, best_sc):
    a = _normalize(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _exact_score(a)

    rv = a[::-1]
    sc = _exact_score(rv)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
        best_sc = sc
        best_a = rv.copy()

    maxv = float(best_a.max())
    if maxv <= 0.0:
        return best_sc, best_a

    fracs = (0.0, 1e-12, 1e-11, 1e-10, 1e-9, 5e-9, 1e-8, 5e-8, 1e-7, 5e-7, 1e-6, 5e-6, 1e-5, 5e-5, 1e-4)
    for frac in fracs:
        b = best_a.copy()
        if frac > 0.0:
            b[b < frac * maxv] = 0.0
        b = _normalize(b)

        sc = _exact_score(b)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = b.copy()

        rv = b[::-1]
        sc2 = _exact_score(rv)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc2
            best_a = rv.copy()

    return best_sc, best_a


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    global _STOP_DEADLINE

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s) - 45.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent = None
    h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if h is not None:
        try:
            p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if p.size >= 32 and p.sum() > 1e-12:
                parent = _normalize(p)
        except Exception:
            parent = None

    parent_len = int(parent.size) if parent is not None else 0
    print("parent present: {} len={}".format(parent is not None, parent_len))

    total_budget = max(60.0, deadline - t0)

    if parent is not None:
        pl = parent_len
        if 6144 <= pl <= 20000:
            main_len = int(pl)
        elif pl > 20000:
            main_len = 20000
        else:
            main_len = 10240
    else:
        main_len = 10240

    if total_budget > 1900.0:
        small_len = 8192
        large_len = 32768
    elif total_budget > 1200.0:
        small_len = 6144
        large_len = 24576
    else:
        small_len = 4096
        large_len = 16384

    mid_len = max(12288, (main_len + large_len) // 2)
    lengths = []
    for L in (small_len, main_len, mid_len, large_len):
        L = int(max(4096, min(32768, L)))
        if L not in lengths:
            lengths.append(L)
    if len(lengths) > 4:
        lengths = lengths[:4]

    print("lengths: {}".format(lengths))

    # Phase 1: low-resolution global shape search.
    n_shape = 4096
    phase1_dead = t0 + min(330.0, max(90.0, total_budget * 0.16))
    lp_time = min(90.0, max(20.0, (phase1_dead - t0) * 0.30))
    lowshape_dead = phase1_dead - lp_time

    lowshape, lowshape_sc = _lowdim_shape_search(n_shape, lowshape_dead, rng, parent)
    print("lowdim shape score: {:.8f}".format(lowshape_sc))

    if time.monotonic() < phase1_dead - 5.0:
        lp_dead = min(phase1_dead, time.monotonic() + lp_time)
        lp_sc, lp_seq = _lp_improve(lowshape, lp_dead, n_target=2048, max_rows=512,
                                    max_iters=8, label="phase1")
        lp_up = _resample(lp_seq, n_shape)
        lp_up_sc = _exact_score(lp_up)
        if np.isfinite(lp_up_sc) and lp_up_sc < lowshape_sc:
            lowshape = lp_up
            lowshape_sc = lp_up_sc
            print("LP improved lowdim: {:.8f}".format(lowshape_sc))

    # Build full-resolution starts from parent / lowdim / structured inits.
    starts = []
    for L in lengths:
        tops = _quick_top_inits(L, parent, rng, topk=3, cap=30)
        arrs = []
        if parent is not None:
            pa = _resample(parent, L)
            arrs.append(pa)
            arrs.append(pa[::-1])
        la = _resample(lowshape, L)
        arrs.append(la)
        arrs.append(la[::-1])
        arrs.extend(tops)

        scored = []
        for arr in arrs:
            arr = _normalize(arr)
            if arr.size != L:
                continue
            s = _exact_score(arr)
            if not np.isfinite(s):
                continue
            dup = False
            for _, pa in scored:
                if np.linalg.norm(arr - pa) < 0.01:
                    dup = True
                    break
            if not dup:
                scored.append((s, arr))

        scored.sort(key=_pair_key)
        for s, arr in scored[:2]:
            starts.append((L, arr, s))

    if not starts:
        L = lengths[0]
        a = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / L
        starts.append((L, a, _exact_score(a)))

    starts.sort(key=_triple_key)

    kept = []
    kept_lens = []
    for item in starts:
        L = item[0]
        if L not in kept_lens:
            kept.append(item)
            kept_lens.append(L)
    for item in starts:
        if len(kept) >= 5:
            break
        L = item[0]
        present = False
        for kl in kept_lens:
            if kl == L:
                present = True
                break
        if not present:
            kept.append(item)
            kept_lens.append(L)
    starts = kept[:5]

    best_sc = np.inf
    best_a = starts[0][1].copy()
    for _, a, s in starts:
        if s < best_sc:
            best_sc = s
            best_a = a.copy()

    print("initial best: {:.8f} len={}".format(best_sc, best_a.size))

    # Phase 2: full-resimum log-LSE L-BFGS trajectories.
    final_reserve = max(220.0, total_budget * 0.20)
    phase2_dead = deadline - final_reserve
    if phase2_dead < t0 + 180.0:
        phase2_dead = deadline - 60.0
    main_total = max(10.0, phase2_dead - t0)

    costs = [float(np.log2(max(8.0, L)) * L) for L, _, _ in starts]
    total_cost = sum(costs)
    weights = [c / total_cost if total_cost > 0 else 1.0 / len(starts) for c in costs]
    capped = [min(w, 0.45) for w in weights]
    cw = sum(capped)
    capped = [w / cw if cw > 0 else 1.0 / len(capped) for w in capped]

    cum = 0.0
    for i, (L, a, _s) in enumerate(starts):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if i == len(starts) - 1:
            fraction = max(0.0, 1.0 - cum)
        else:
            fraction = capped[i] if cw > 0 else 1.0 / max(1, len(starts))
        sub_dead = t0 + (cum + fraction) * main_total
        sub_dead = min(sub_dead, phase2_dead)
        cum += max(0.0, min(1.0, fraction))

        if now > sub_dead - 25.0:
            continue

        sc, a2 = _lbfgs_log_optimize(a, sub_dead, "traj{} len={}".format(i + 1, L))
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a2.copy()
            print("after traj {} len {}: {:.8f}".format(i + 1, L, best_sc))

    best_a = _normalize(best_a)

    # Phase 3: final intensive improvement.
    final_dead = deadline - 20.0

    rem = final_dead - time.monotonic()
    if best_a.size < 16384 and rem > 430.0:
        target_len = 16384
        up_dead = min(final_dead - 180.0, time.monotonic() + min(240.0, rem - 220.0))
        if up_dead > time.monotonic() + 40.0:
            up = _resample(best_a, target_len)
            sc, a2 = _lbfgs_log_optimize(
                up,
                up_dead,
                "upsample to {}".format(target_len),
                betas=(20000.0, 100000.0, 500000.0, 2000000.0),
            )
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = a2.copy()
                print("upsample improved: {:.8f}".format(best_sc))

    rem = final_dead - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 130.0:
        final_dead2 = final_dead - 55.0
        if final_dead2 > time.monotonic() + 40.0:
            sc, a2 = _lbfgs_log_optimize(
                best_a,
                final_dead2,
                "final log",
                betas=(100000.0, 500000.0, 2000000.0, 8000000.0),
            )
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = a2.copy()
                print("final log improved: {:.8f}".format(best_sc))

    rem = final_dead2 - time.monotonic() if 'final_dead2' in locals() else final_dead - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 70.0:
        sc, a2 = _polish_exact(best_a, min(final_dead - 18.0, final_dead - 25.0), "polish")
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a2.copy()
            print("polish improved: {:.8f}".format(best_sc))

    best_sc, best_a = _cleanup_best(best_a, best_sc)

    out_arr = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out_arr = np.nan_to_num(out_arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(out_arr.sum())
    if S < 0.01:
        out_arr = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
    else:
        out_arr = out_arr / S
        out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)

    out = [float(x) for x in out_arr]
    internal_score = _exact_score(out)
    verified = internal_score
    if len(out) <= 20000 and time.monotonic() < t0 + float(budget_s) - 5.0:
        try:
            verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
        except Exception:
            verified = internal_score

    print("FINAL internal={:.8f} verified={:.8f} len={} time={:.1f}s".format(
        internal_score, verified, len(out), time.monotonic() - t0
    ))
    return out
```
